[31m+ /** Retorna itens visíveis numa dada plataforma, ordenados por `order` (asc). */[39m
[31m+ export function itemsForPlatform(platform: NavPlatform): NavItem[] {[39m
[31m+   const filtered = navigation.filter((n) => n.platforms.includes(platform));[39m
[31m+   return filtered.sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 999) - (b.order ?? 999));[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** Retorna todos os itens desktop agrupados por `section`, respeitando `DESKTOP_SECTION_ORDER` e `order`. */[39m
[31m+ export function desktopItemsBySection(): Array<{ section: NavSection; items: NavItem[] }> {[39m
[31m+   const bySection = new Map<NavSection, NavItem[]>();[39m
[31m+   for (const item of itemsForPlatform("desktop")) {[39m
[31m+     const arr = bySection.get(item.section) ?? [];[39m
[31m+     arr.push(item);[39m
[31m+     bySection.set(item.section, arr);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   return DESKTOP_SECTION_ORDER[39m
[31m+     .map((section) => ({[39m
[31m+       section,[39m
[31m+       items: (bySection.get(section) ?? []).sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 999) - (b.order ?? 999)),[39m
[31m+     }))[39m
[31m+     .filter((g) => g.items.length > 0);[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** Retorna itens visíveis numa dada plataforma, ordenados por `order` (asc). */[39m
[31m+ export function itemsForPlatform(platform: NavPlatform): NavItem[] {[39m
[31m+   const filtered = navigation.filter((n) => n.platforms.includes(platform));[39m
[31m+   return filtered.sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 999) - (b.order ?? 999));[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** Retorna todos os itens desktop agrupados por `section`, respeitando `DESKTOP_SECTION_ORDER` e `order`. */[39m
[31m+ export function desktopItemsBySection(): Array<{ section: NavSection; items: NavItem[] }> {[39m
[31m+   const bySection = new Map<NavSection, NavItem[]>();[39m
[31m+   for (const item of itemsForPlatform("desktop")) {[39m
[31m+     const arr = bySection.get(item.section) ?? [];[39m
[31m+     arr.push(item);[39m
[31m+     bySection.set(item.section, arr);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   return DESKTOP_SECTION_ORDER[39m
[31m+     .map((section) => ({[39m
[31m+       section,[39m
[31m+       items: (bySection.get(section) ?? []).sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 999) - (b.order ?? 999)),[39m
[31m+     }))[39m
[31m+     .filter((g) => g.items.length > 0);[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ import { ResponsiveTable, MobileCard } from "@/components/ResponsiveHelpers";[39m
[31m+ import { useIsMobile } from "@/hooks/use-mobile";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ import { ProdutosTab } from "@/components/protocolos/ProdutosTab";[39m
[31m+ import { CatalogoTecnicoTab } from "@/components/protocolos/CatalogoTecnicoTab";[39m
[31m+ import { DeleteProtocoloButton } from "@/components/protocolos/DeleteProtocoloButton";[39m
[31m+ import { useCulturas } from "@/hooks/useCulturas";[39m
[31m+ import { CatalogOriginBadge, OwnedCatalogItemControls } from "@/components/protocolos/OwnedCatalogItemControls";[39m
[31m+ import { OwnedCatalogItemDialog } from "@/components/protocolos/OwnedCatalogItemDialog";[39m
[31m+ import { OwnedCatalogConfirmDialog } from "@/components/protocolos/OwnedCatalogConfirmDialog";[39m
[31m+ import { useOwnedCatalogCapability } from "@/hooks/useOwnedCatalogMutations";[39m
[31m+ import { useOwnedCatalogRead } from "@/hooks/useOwnedCatalogRead";[39m
[31m+ import { OwnedCatalogPagination } from "@/components/protocolos/OwnedCatalogPagination";[39m
[31m+ import { TENANT_OWNED_CATALOG_ENABLED } from "@/lib/catalogo/tenantCatalogOwnership";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // --- Culturas ---[39m
[31m+ // Culturas são base GLOBAL. Escrita de cliente foi revogada no banco[39m
[31m+ // (global_culturas_client_write_containment); não existe writer servidor[39m
[31m+ // auditado. Esta aba é somente consulta para todos os perfis.[39m
[31m+ export function CulturasTab() {[39m
[31m+   const [showArchived, setShowArchived] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [ownedOffset, setOwnedOffset] = useState(0);[39m
[31m+   const [search, setSearch] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const ownedRead = useOwnedCatalogRead("cultura", { archived: showArchived, offset: ownedOffset, search });[39m
[31m+   const { data: items, isLoading, isError, error, refetch, isFetching } = useCulturas();[39m
[31m+   const allRows = useMemo(() => [...(items ?? []).filter((item) => !item.owner_organization_id), ...(ownedRead.data?.items ?? [])][39m
[31m+     .filter((item) => (showArchived || !item.archived_at) && item.nome.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())), [items, ownedRead.data, showArchived, search]);[39m
[31m+   const tenantVis = useManagedVisibility("cultura", allRows);[39m
[31m+   const canManageVis = useCanManageTenantVisibility();[39m
[31m+   const rows = tenantVis.displayed;[39m
[31m+   const ownedCapability = useOwnedCatalogCapability();[39m
[31m+   const [ownedDialog, setOwnedDialog] = useState<{ open: boolean; item: (typeof allRows)[number] | null }>({ open: false, item: null });[39m
[31m+   const [ownedConfirm, setOwnedConfirm] = useState<{ item: (typeof allRows)[number]; operation: "archive" | "restore" } | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-4">[39m
[31m+       <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground" data-testid="culturas-readonly-notice">[39m
[31m+         Culturas disponíveis para a sua empresa.[39m
[31m+       </p>[39m
[31m+       {TENANT_OWNED_CATALOG_ENABLED && ownedCapability.canManage && ([39m
[31m+         <div><Button size="sm" onClick={() => setOwnedDialog({ open: true, item: null })}><Plus className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" />Cultura própria</Button></div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       <Input aria-label="Buscar culturas" value={search} onChange={(event) => { setSearch(event.target.value); setOwnedOffset(0); }} placeholder="Buscar culturas" />[39m
[31m+       <TenantVisibilityBar v={tenantVis} />[39m
[31m+       {TENANT_OWNED_CATALOG_ENABLED && <OwnedCatalogPagination entity="cultura" archived={showArchived} onArchived={setShowArchived}[39m
[31m+         offset={ownedOffset} onOffset={setOwnedOffset} hasMore={ownedRead.data?.has_more ?? false} busy={ownedRead.isFetching}[39m
[31m+         error={ownedRead.isError ? (ownedRead.error as Error).message : null} />}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {isLoading ? ([39m
[31m+         <div className="py-8 text-center text-muted-foreground" role="status">Carregando culturas...</div>[39m
[31m+       ) : isError ? ([39m
[31m+         <div className="py-8 text-center space-y-3" role="alert">[39m
[31m+           <p className="text-sm text-destructive">[39m
[31m+             Não foi possível carregar as culturas. {(error as Error)?.message}[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m
[31m+           <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => refetch()} disabled={isFetching}>[39m
[31m+             Tentar novamente[39m
[31m+           </Button>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       ) : ([39m
[31m+       <ResponsiveTable[39m
[31m+         mobileCards={[39m
[31m+           rows.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+             <div className="text-center text-muted-foreground py-8">Nenhuma cultura cadastrada</div>[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             rows.map((c) => ([39m
[31m+               <MobileCard[39m
[31m+                 key={c.id}[39m
[31m+                 title={<div className="flex items-center gap-2">{c.nome}<CatalogOriginBadge item={c} /></div>}[39m
[31m+                 actions={<>[39m
[31m+                   {canManageVis && !c.owner_organization_id && <TenantVisibilityToggle entity="cultura" item={{ id: c.id, nome: c.nome, ativo: c.ativo }}[39m
[31m+                     state={tenantVis.map?.get(c.id)} disabledReason={tenantVis.error ?? (tenantVis.map ? null : "Carregando preferências.")} />}[39m
[31m+                   <OwnedCatalogItemControls item={c} actorOrganizationId={ownedCapability.scope?.organizationId} canManage={ownedCapability.canManage}[39m
[31m+                     onEdit={() => setOwnedDialog({ open: true, item: c })} onArchive={() => setOwnedConfirm({ item: c, operation: "archive" })}[39m
[31m+                     onRestore={() => setOwnedConfirm({ item: c, operation: "restore" })} />[39m
[31m+                 </>}[39m
[31m+                 content={[[39m
[31m+                   { label: "Descrição", value: c.descricao || "—" },[39m
[31m+                   { label: "Situação", value: c.ativo ? "Ativa" : "Inativa" },[39m
[31m+                   ...(tenantVis.map?.get(c.id)?.hidden ? [{ label: "Empresa", value: "Oculta nesta empresa" }] : []),[39m
[31m+                 ]}[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             ))[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <Table>[39m
[31m+           <TableHeader><TableRow><TableHead>Cultura</TableHead><TableHead>Descrição</TableHead><TableHead>Situação</TableHead>{canManageVis && <TableHead className="text-right">Empresa</TableHead>}</TableRow></TableHeader>[39m
[31m+           <TableBody>[39m
[31m+             {rows.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+               <TableRow><TableCell colSpan={canManageVis ? 4 : 3} className="text-center text-muted-foreground">Nenhuma cultura cadastrada</TableCell></TableRow>[39m
[31m+             ) : rows.map((c) => ([39m
[31m+               <TableRow key={c.id}>[39m
[31m+                 <TableCell className="font-medium"><div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">{c.nome}<CatalogOriginBadge item={c} /></div></TableCell>[39m
[31m+                 <TableCell className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{c.descricao || "—"}</TableCell>[39m
[31m+                 <TableCell>[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex items-center gap-1 flex-wrap">[39m
[31m+                     <Badge variant={c.ativo ? "default" : "secondary"}>{c.ativo ? "Ativa" : "Inativa"}</Badge>[39m
[31m+                     <TenantHiddenBadge state={tenantVis.map?.get(c.id)} />[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 </TableCell>[39m
[31m+                 {canManageVis && ([39m
[31m+                   <TableCell className="text-right">[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex justify-end gap-1">[39m
[31m+                       {!c.owner_organization_id && <TenantVisibilityToggle entity="cultura" item={{ id: c.id, nome: c.nome, ativo: c.ativo }}[39m
[31m+                         state={tenantVis.map?.get(c.id)} disabledReason={tenantVis.error ?? (tenantVis.map ? null : "Carregando preferências.")} />} [39m
[31m+                       <OwnedCatalogItemControls item={c} actorOrganizationId={ownedCapability.scope?.organizationId} canManage={ownedCapability.canManage}[39m
[31m+                         onEdit={() => setOwnedDialog({ open: true, item: c })}[39m
[31m+                         onArchive={() => setOwnedConfirm({ item: c, operation: "archive" })}[39m
[31m+                         onRestore={() => setOwnedConfirm({ item: c, operation: "restore" })} />[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   </TableCell>[39m
[31m+                 )}[39m
[31m+               </TableRow>[39m
[31m+             ))}[39m
[31m+           </TableBody>[39m
[31m+         </Table>[39m
[31m+       </ResponsiveTable>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       <OwnedCatalogItemDialog open={ownedDialog.open} onOpenChange={(open) => setOwnedDialog((current) => ({ ...current, open }))}[39m
[31m+         entity="cultura" item={ownedDialog.item} onApplied={async () => { await ownedRead.refetch(); await refetch(); }}[39m
[31m+         onLocateGlobal={(id) => { setOwnedDialog({ open: false, item: null }); tenantVis.setShowHidden(true); const match = allRows.find((row) => row.id === id); if (match) setSearch(match.nome); }} />[39m
[31m+       <OwnedCatalogConfirmDialog item={ownedConfirm?.item ?? null} entity="cultura" operation={ownedConfirm?.operation ?? "archive"}[39m
[31m+         onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setOwnedConfirm(null)} onApplied={async () => { await ownedRead.refetch(); await refetch(); }} />[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // --- Problemas (Catálogo unificado) ---[39m
[31m+ import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";[39m
[31m+ import { rowClickProps, stopRowClick } from "@/lib/rowClick";[39m
[31m+ import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";[39m
[31m+ import { Search, Pencil, Filter, CheckCircle2, XCircle } from "lucide-react";[39m
[31m+ import { DialogFooter } from "@/components/ui/dialog";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const CATEGORIA_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {[39m
[31m+   insetos_praga: "Pragas",[39m
[31m+   doencas_fungicas: "Doenças fúngicas",[39m
[31m+   doencas_bacterianas: "Doenças bacterianas",[39m
[31m+   nematoides: "Nematoides",[39m
[31m+   plantas_daninhas: "Plantas daninhas",[39m
[31m+   desequilibrio_biologico_solo: "Solo",[39m
[31m+   baixa_vitalidade: "Nutricional",[39m
[31m+   fitotoxidade: "Falha operacional",[39m
[31m+   outro: "Outros",[39m
[31m+ };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export function ProblemasTab() {[39m
[31m+   const isMobile = useIsMobile();[39m
[31m+   const queryClient = useQueryClient();[39m
[31m+   const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [catFilter, setCatFilter] = useState("todas");[39m
[31m+   const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("todos");[39m
[31m+   const [dialogOpen, setDialogOpen] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [editingItem, setEditingItem] = useState<any>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [formData, setFormData] = useState({[39m
[31m+     nome: "",[39m
[31m+     nome_cientifico: "",[39m
[31m+     categoria: "outro",[39m
[31m+     ativo: true,[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const { data: problemas, isLoading } = useQuery({[39m
[31m+     queryKey: ["catalogo-problemas-admin"],[39m
[31m+     queryFn: async () => {[39m
[31m+       const { data, error } = await supabase[39m
[31m+         .from("catalogo_problemas")[39m
[31m+         .select("*")[39m
[31m+         .order("nome");[39m
[31m+       if (error) throw error;[39m
[31m+       return data;[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const saveMutation = useMutation({[39m
[31m+     mutationFn: async (payload: any) => {[39m
[31m+       assertGlobalCatalogWriteBlocked();[39m
[31m+       if (editingItem) {[39m
[31m+         const { error } = await supabase[39m
[31m+           .from("catalogo_problemas")[39m
[31m+           .update(payload)[39m
[31m+           .eq("id", editingItem.id);[39m
[31m+         if (error) throw error;[39m
[31m+       } else {[39m
[31m+         const { error } = await supabase[39m
[31m+           .from("catalogo_problemas")[39m
[31m+           .insert([payload]);[39m
[31m+         if (error) throw error;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+     onSuccess: () => {[39m
[31m+       queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["catalogo-problemas-admin"] });[39m
[31m+       queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["catalogo-problemas-ativo"] });[39m
[31m+       toast.success(editingItem ? "Atualizado!" : "Cadastrado!");[39m
[31m+       setDialogOpen(false);[39m
[31m+       resetForm();[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+     onError: (err: any) => toast.error(err.message),[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const toggleAtivoMutation = useMutation({[39m
[31m+     mutationFn: async ({ id, ativo }: { id: string; ativo: boolean }) => {[39m
[31m+       assertGlobalCatalogWriteBlocked();[39m
[31m+       const { error } = await supabase[39m
[31m+         .from("catalogo_problemas")[39m
[31m+         .update({ ativo })[39m
[31m+         .eq("id", id);[39m
[31m+       if (error) throw error;[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+     onSuccess: () => {[39m
[31m+       queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["catalogo-problemas-admin"] });[39m
[31m+       queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["catalogo-problemas-ativo"] });[39m
[31m+       toast.success("Status atualizado");[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const resetForm = () => {[39m
[31m+     setFormData({ nome: "", nome_cientifico: "", categoria: "outro", ativo: true });[39m
[31m+     setEditingItem(null);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleEdit = (item: any) => {[39m
[31m+     if (!TENANT_CAN_EDIT_GLOBAL_CATALOG) return;[39m
[31m+     setEditingItem(item);[39m
[31m+     setFormData({[39m
[31m+       nome: item.nome,[39m
[31m+       nome_cientifico: item.nome_cientifico || "",[39m
[31m+       categoria: item.categoria,[39m
[31m+       ativo: item.ativo,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     setDialogOpen(true);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const filtered = (problemas || []).filter((p: any) => {[39m
[31m+     const s = searchTerm.toLowerCase();[39m
[31m+     const matchesSearch =[39m
[31m+       p.nome.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||[39m
[31m+       (p.nome_cientifico?.toLowerCase().includes(s));[39m
[31m+     const matchesCat = catFilter === "todas" || p.categoria === catFilter;[39m
[31m+     const matchesStatus =[39m
[31m+       statusFilter === "todos" ||[39m
[31m+       (statusFilter === "ativos" && p.ativo) ||[39m
[31m+       (statusFilter === "inativos" && !p.ativo);[39m
[31m+     return matchesSearch && matchesCat && matchesStatus;[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="space-y-4">[39m
[31m+       <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 justify-between">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 flex-1">[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex-1 min-w-[240px] relative">[39m
[31m+             <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />[39m
[31m+             <Input[39m
[31m+               placeholder="Buscar por nome ou nome científico..."[39m
[31m+               className="pl-9"[39m
[31m+               value={searchTerm}[39m
[31m+               onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}[39m
[31m+             />[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <div className="w-52">[39m
[31m+             <Select value={catFilter} onValueChange={setCatFilter}>[39m
[31m+               <SelectTrigger>[39m
[31m+                 <Filter className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />[39m
[31m+                 <SelectValue placeholder="Categoria" />[39m
[31m+               </SelectTrigger>[39m
[31m+               <SelectContent>[39m
[31m+                 <SelectItem value="todas">Todas Categorias</SelectItem>[39m
[31m+                 {Object.entries(CATEGORIA_LABELS).map(([v, l]) => ([39m
[31m+                   <SelectItem key={v} value={v}>{l}</SelectItem>[39m
[31m+                 ))}[39m
[31m+               </SelectContent>[39m
[31m+             </Select>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <div className="w-40">[39m
[31m+             <Select value={statusFilter} onValueChange={setStatusFilter}>[39m
[31m+               <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Status" /></SelectTrigger>[39m
[31m+               <SelectContent>[39m
[31m+                 <SelectItem value="todos">Todos Status</SelectItem>[39m
[31m+                 <SelectItem value="ativos">Apenas Ativos</SelectItem>[39m
[31m+                 <SelectItem value="inativos">Apenas Inativos</SelectItem>[39m
[31m+               </SelectContent>[39m
[31m+             </Select>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         {TENANT_CAN_EDIT_GLOBAL_CATALOG && ([39m
[31m+         <Button size="sm" onClick={() => { resetForm(); setDialogOpen(true); }}>[39m
[31m+           <Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" /> Novo Problema[39m
[31m+         </Button>[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <ResponsiveTable[39m
[31m+         mobileCards={[39m
[31m+           isLoading ? ([39m
[31m+             <div className="text-center py-8">Carregando...</div>[39m
[31m+           ) : filtered.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+             <div className="text-center py-8 text-muted-foreground italic">Nenhum problema encontrado</div>[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             filtered.map((p: any) => ([39m
[31m+               <MobileCard[39m
[31m+                 key={p.id}[39m
[31m+                 ariaLabel={p.nome}[39m
[31m+                 title={p.nome}[39m
[31m+                 subtitle={p.nome_cientifico}[39m
[31m+                 status={[39m
[31m+                   <Badge[39m
[31m+                     variant={p.ativo ? "default" : "secondary"}[39m
[31m+                     className={cn([39m
[31m+                       "text-[10px]",[39m
[31m+                       p.ativo[39m
[31m+                         ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700 hover:bg-green-200 border-green-200"[39m
[31m+                         : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 border-gray-200 italic"[39m
[31m+                     )}[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     {p.ativo ? "Ativo" : "Inativo"}[39m
[31m+                   </Badge>[39m
[31m+                 }[39m
[31m+                 content={[{ label: "Categoria", value: CATEGORIA_LABELS[p.categoria] || p.categoria }]}[39m
[31m+                 actions={!TENANT_CAN_EDIT_GLOBAL_CATALOG ? undefined :[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex w-full gap-2" onClick={stopRowClick}>[39m
[31m+                     <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="flex-1" onClick={() => handleEdit(p)}>[39m
[31m+                       <Pencil className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" /> Editar[39m
[31m+                     </Button>[39m
[31m+                     <Button[39m
[31m+                       variant="outline"[39m
[31m+                       size="sm"[39m
[31m+                       className="flex-1"[39m
[31m+                       onClick={() => toggleAtivoMutation.mutate({ id: p.id, ativo: !p.ativo })}[39m
[31m+                     >[39m
[31m+                       {p.ativo ? ([39m
[31m+                         <><XCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 text-destructive" /> Inativar</>[39m
[31m+                       ) : ([39m
[31m+                         <><CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 text-primary" /> Ativar</>[39m
[31m+                       )}[39m
[31m+                     </Button>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 }[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             ))[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <Table>[39m
[31m+           <TableHeader>[39m
[31m+             <TableRow>[39m
[31m+               <TableHead>Problema</TableHead>[39m
[31m+               <TableHead>Categoria</TableHead>[39m
[31m+               <TableHead>Status</TableHead>[39m
[31m+               <TableHead className="text-right">Ações</TableHead>[39m
[31m+             </TableRow>[39m
[31m+           </TableHeader>[39m
[31m+           <TableBody>[39m
[31m+             {isLoading ? ([39m
[31m+               <TableRow><TableCell colSpan={4} className="text-center py-8">Carregando...</TableCell></TableRow>[39m
[31m+             ) : filtered.length === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+               <TableRow><TableCell colSpan={4} className="text-center py-8 text-muted-foreground">Nenhum problema cadastrado</TableCell></TableRow>[39m
[31m+             ) : ([39m
[31m+               filtered.map((p: any) => ([39m
[31m+                 <TableRow key={p.id} {...(TENANT_CAN_EDIT_GLOBAL_CATALOG ? rowClickProps(() => handleEdit(p)) : {})}>[39m
[31m+                   <TableCell>[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex flex-col">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="font-medium">{p.nome}</span>[39m
[31m+                       {p.nome_cientifico && ([39m

[31m+                     <Button[39m

[31m+           </DialogHeader>[39m
[31m+           <div className="space-y-4 py-4">[39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-2">[39m

[31m+               <Input[39m

[31m+               />[39m
